Third-party licences for Kurasi 0.4.0

Kurasi itself is under the MIT License (LICENSE.txt next to this file). kurasi.exe links the 373 Rust crates
listed below. Each is used under the licence named in the second column; where a crate offers a
choice, Kurasi uses the first of: MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD-3-Clause, BSD-2-Clause. The texts follow the list.

pdfium.dll is a separate file. Its licences are in the folder licenses\pdfium.
The installer carries Microsoft's WebView2 Evergreen Bootstrapper (MicrosoftEdgeWebview2Setup.exe).
When the WebView2 Runtime is missing, the bootstrapper installs it under Microsoft's terms.
The last section, "Components outside Cargo.lock", lists the 6 components of kurasi.exe and
of the installer that are absent from Cargo.lock, with their licence texts.

==============================================================================
Crates with prebuilt binaries in their source
==============================================================================
The source folders of these crates hold prebuilt .lib, .dll, .a or .so files:
  libloading 0.9.0, dependency of kurasi.exe: tests\nagisa32.dll, tests\nagisa64.dll
  vcpkg 0.2.15, build or dev dependency: test-data\no-status\installed\x64-windows\bin\boost_atomic-vc141-mt-x64-1_67.dll, test-data\no-status\installed\x64-windows\bin\boost_chrono-vc141-mt-x64-1_67.dll and 339 more
  webview2-com-sys 0.39.1, dependency of kurasi.exe: arm64\WebView2Loader.dll, arm64\WebView2Loader.dll.lib and 7 more; entry under "Components outside Cargo.lock"
  windows_x86_64_msvc 0.53.1, dependency of kurasi.exe: lib\windows.0.53.0.lib

    a separate file or files, that is not Covered Software.

    means this document.

Crates of the Rust library workspace
------------------------------------

COPYRIGHT-library.html lists 141 out-of-tree crates of the Rust library workspace. The list
covers every target of the standard library and the tools of the workspace. The rlib files of
the target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc, the target of kurasi.exe, contain cfg-if 1.0.4, hashbrown
0.17.1, rustc-demangle 0.1.28 and windows-link 0.2.1 for std, getopts 0.2.24 for the test
harness and rustc-literal-escaper 0.0.8 for proc_macro. The notices of every listed crate
follow. Each crate is listed with its version and its licence as COPYRIGHT-library.html gives
them.

Kurasi uses MIT for every crate that offers it. The other crates: foldhash 0.1.5 and foldhash
0.2.0 under Zlib; fortanix-sgx-abi 0.6.1 under MPL-2.0; ryu 1.0.23 under Apache-2.0; syscalls
0.6.18 under BSD-2-Clause. unicode-ident 1.0.24 adds Unicode-3.0 to MIT.

Crates inside nsis_tauri_utils.dll
----------------------------------

nsis_tauri_utils.dll is the crate nsis-tauri-utils 0.5.3 built for i686-pc-windows-msvc as a
no_std library. Besides its own code and the workspace crate nsis-plugin-api (MIT OR Apache-2.0,
the LICENSE_MIT above), it contains core and alloc of the Rust standard library (licence in the
entry "Rust standard library (std, core, alloc)") and the three crates below. The proc-macro
crate nsis-fn and its dependencies proc-macro2, quote, syn and unicode-ident run at build time
only. The versions are the newest releases on crates.io that matched the requirements of the
workspace's Cargo.toml files on 26 Jan 2026, the day the DLL was built: semver "1.0" with
default features off, windows-sys "0.61.2", and windows-link "^0.2.1" through windows-sys.
Kurasi uses MIT for each.

semver 1.0.27                               MIT OR Apache-2.0
windows-link 0.2.1                          MIT OR Apache-2.0
windows-sys 0.61.2                          MIT OR Apache-2.0

person obtaining a copy of this software and associated
documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the
Software without restriction, including without
limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge,
publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of
the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software
is furnished to do so, subject to the following
conditions:

The above copyright notice and this permission notice
shall be included in all copies or substantial portions
of the Software.

THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED
TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A
PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT
SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY
CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR

DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NSIS LZMA compression module (LZMA decompressor of the installer stub)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Version:   NSIS 3.13
Ships in:  Kurasi-<version>-Setup.exe and the uninstall.exe it installs (the installer is
           built with "compression": "lzma")
Licence:   CPL-1.0 with the NSIS special exception for the LZMA compression module
Text:      COPYING of NSIS 3.13: COMMON PUBLIC LICENSE VERSION 1.0, SPECIAL EXCEPTION FOR
           LZMA COMPRESSION MODULE

The installer and the uninstaller of Kurasi contain the LZMA decompressor of the
NSIS LZMA compression module in object code form. Kurasi uses that module unmodified, as
shipped with NSIS 3.13, and adds no Contribution of its own to it. Section 3 of the Common
Public License version 1.0 sets the terms for distribution in object code form, and the
distributor of Kurasi meets them as follows:

  (i)   On behalf of all Contributors to the LZMA compression module, the distributor of
        Kurasi disclaims all warranties and conditions, express and implied, including
        warranties or conditions of title and non-infringement, and implied warranties or
        conditions of merchantability and fitness for a particular purpose (section 5).
  (ii)  On behalf of all Contributors, the distributor of Kurasi excludes all liability for
        damages, including direct, indirect, special, incidental and consequential damages,
        such as lost profits (section 6).
  (iii) Any provisions of the Kurasi licence that differ from the Common Public License are
        offered by the distributor of Kurasi alone.
  (iv)  The source code of the LZMA compression module, as shipped with NSIS 3.13, is
        available from the distributor of this copy of Kurasi on request, and also from the
        NSIS 3.13 source package, nsis-3.13-src.tar.bz2, linked from
        https://nsis.sourceforge.io/Download
        (https://prdownloads.sourceforge.net/nsis/nsis-3.13-src.tar.bz2?download).

Under the special exception granted by Igor Pavlov and Amir Szekely, the authors of the
module, the installer script and the other code linked to the module keep their own
licences. The licence and the exception follow as COPYING of NSIS 3.13 states them.
